### [1] [Sol Video Inference Engine: Agent-Native Full-Stack Acceleration Framework for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23743)
*Yitong Li,Junsong Chen,Haopeng Li,Haozhe Liu,Jincheng Yu,Ligeng Zhu,Ping Luo,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sol Video Inference Engine是一个智能体驱动的、无需训练的视频扩散模型加速框架，通过并行技能智能体对缓存、稀疏注意力、token剪枝、量化和核融合五种技术进行实例特定优化，在三个不同规模的视频模型（64B、22B、2B）上实现2倍以上端到端加速且保持近乎无损的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型随规模扩大推理成本剧增，而最有效的加速策略高度依赖于具体实例（模型、硬件、推理配置的组合），不同模型在架构、数值敏感性和注意力集中模式上存在差异，推理设置和硬件平台也各不相同，导致手动性能工程调优空间巨大且成本高昂。

Method: 提出Sol Video Inference Engine，将五种通用加速技术（缓存、稀疏注意力、token剪枝、量化、核融合）组织成智能体加速栈。针对具体的部署目标（模型+硬件+服务配置），并行技能智能体分别优化各技术实现，智能体集成器将其组合成全局加速栈，人工验证器提供生成质量反馈。

Result: 在64B Cosmos3-Super、22B LTX-2.3和2B SANA-Video三个不同规模和架构的视频模型上，完整加速栈实现了2倍以上的端到端加速，同时VBench质量指标保持近乎无损。

Conclusion: 该智能体框架能够以极少的人力成本实现视频扩散模型的高效实例特定加速，证明了智能体驱动的自动化加速方案在视频扩散领域的有效性。

Abstract: Modern video diffusion models achieve higher generation quality through scaling, but this also increases inference cost. Although many acceleration methods have been proposed, a central challenge is that the most effective acceleration strategy is highly instance-specific: a recipe that works well for one combination of model, hardware, and inference configuration often does not transfer to another. Different models vary in architecture, numerical sensitivity, and attention concentration patterns. Inference settings differ in spatial and temporal resolution and video duration, while hardware platforms differ in memory hierarchy, supported numerical formats, and kernel throughput. These factors create a large tuning space, making manual performance engineering costly. We present Sol Video Inference Engine, an agentic, native, training-free acceleration framework for video diffusion models. It organizes five broadly applicable techniques, cache, sparse attention, token pruning, quantization, and kernel fusion, into an agentic acceleration stack for instance-specific optimization. For a concrete deployment target defined by a model, hardware platform, and serving configuration, parallel skill agents optimize the implementation of each technique, an agent integrator composes them into a global acceleration stack, and a human validator provides feedback on generation quality. We instantiate this workflow on three video models with different sizes and architectures: 64B Cosmos3-Super, 22B LTX-2.3, and 2B SANA-Video. With little human effort, the full stack achieves more than 2x end-to-end acceleration while maintaining near-lossless VBench quality, demonstrating the effectiveness of the agent framework for video diffusion acceleration.

</details>


### [2] [Reflective VLA: In-Context Action Consequences Make VLAs Generalize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25215)
*Qing Lian,Kent Yu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflective VLA，一种通过引入观察-动作-后果三元组上下文来增强VLA模型跨环境泛化能力的方法，在LIBERO-Plus和LIBERO-Plus-Hard上分别提升5.4和4.2个百分点的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型是反应式的，仅根据当前指令和观测预测下一个动作，隐含假设当前观测完全包含动作相关状态。但在具身控制中，机器人标定、相机-机器人几何关系等具身特定因素难以从单帧观测中辨识，导致反应式策略过拟合训练环境、泛化性能差。

Method: Reflective VLA将每个决策条件化为一个观察-动作-后果三元组上下文，记录机器人观测、执行的动作以及场景变化后的结果，暴露部署特定的动作-效果映射。架构上，所有观测模态通过VLM下的共享注意力路由，动作专家直接基于历史三元组和当前观测推理；采用块因果掩码实现并行多帧训练且支持KV缓存实时推理。

Result: 在标准LIBERO和SimplerEnv-Bridge上保持强分布内性能；在分布偏移的LIBERO-Plus和LIBERO-Plus-Hard上，相较于匹配的反应式基线，平均成功率分别提升5.4和4.2个百分点。消融实验表明，动作后果而非单纯的上下文长度是跨环境泛化的关键。

Conclusion: 通过引入动作后果信息，Reflective VLA能有效解决反应式策略在具身控制中因单帧观测无法辨识具身特定因素而导致的泛化问题，为VLA模型在部署环境中的鲁棒性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models are reactive: they predict the next action from the current instruction and observation, implicitly assuming that the current observation fully specifies the action-relevant state. In embodied control, however, embodiment-specific factors such as camera-to-robot geometry, robot calibration, or systematic actuation bias are often hard to identify from a single observation. As a result, reactive policies cannot reliably disambiguate these factors in general, overfitting to training environments and generalizing poorly at deployment. We propose Reflective VLA, which conditions each decision on a context of observation-action-consequence triplets. Each triplet records not only what the robot observed and executed, but also how the scene changed afterward, exposing the deployment-specific mapping from actions to observed effects. Architecturally, Reflective VLA routes all observation modalities through the VLM under shared attention, so the action expert reasons directly over past triplets and the current observation. A block-causal mask enables parallel multi-frame training without leakage and supports KV-cached real-time inference. On standard LIBERO and SimplerEnv-Bridge, Reflective VLA preserves strong in-distribution performance. Under distribution shift on LIBERO-Plus and the harder LIBERO-Plus-Hard, it improves average success rate by 5.4 and 4.2 percentage points over a matched reactive baseline. Ablations with a matched history-only baseline further show that action consequences -- rather than additional context length alone -- are the key to cross-environment generalization. Project page: https://lianqing11.github.io/reflective-vla-page/

</details>


### [3] [Autonomous Video Generation with Counterfactual Controllability for Self-Evolving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24152)
*Xin Wang,Wenxuan Liu,Tongtong Feng,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出视频生成虽被视为世界建模，但仅靠视觉预测无法得到真正可控的具身世界模型。作者提出以反事实可控性为核心的自演进视频生成才是实现世界模型的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献声称"视频生成即世界建模"这一论断虽有建设性，但危险地认为仅靠扩展视觉预测就能自动产生具身智能体。作者希望纠正这一误解，提出更准确的观点。

Method: 提出"反事实可控性"（counterfactual controllability）作为关键判据：模型需要具备回答"在某个动作下会发生什么"的能力，检验生成的未来能否通过具身约束，并将动作知识反馈回未来的生成过程。

Result: 论证了仅靠预测逼真度不足以成为世界模型，核心差距在于能否进行反事实推理和具身可控性检验。

Conclusion: 具备反事实可控性的自主视频生成是实现自演进世界模型的有前途的路径。

Abstract: Existing literature claims that video generation essentially is world modelling. On the one hand, the claim is productive because it pushes generative AI beyond static images and toward temporally extended physical scenes. On the other hand, this claim dangerously relies on the belief that scaling visual prediction alone will automatically yield physical agents. We prefer a more accurate statement: video generation models learn a partial, implicit spatiotemporal world model, but not a fully grounded or controllable one. The reason is as follows: a model may generate a plausible video of a drone crossing a forest or a robot arm manipulating a cup, yet still fail to know which variables are controllable, which constraints belong to a particular body and which futures remain valid under intervention. The frontier in essence is not predictive realism alone, instead it emphasizes a self-evolving generative nature that requires the decisive criterion to be counterfactual controllability: the capability of asking what would happen under an action, to test whether the generated future can survive embodiment constraints and to feed the resulting action knowledge back into future imagination (generation). Therefore, in this paper we present a new perspective, i.e., autonomous video generation with counterfactual controllability is one promising way to realize self-evolving world models.

</details>


### [4] [MotifGen: Spatiotemporal interpolation of misaligned satellite images via multi-source generative modeling, in an application to tropical cyclones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24263)
*Clément Dauvilliers,Claire Monteleoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能够处理多源异构、时间不规则、地理错位的生成模型，用于热带气旋微波图像的空时插值。通过自监督训练结合微波与红外数据，模型在CRPS评分和功率谱方面均优于监督方法和确定性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波卫星图像在监测热带气旋降水和强度方面至关重要，但存在重访周期长、可能错过快速演化阶段的问题，亟需有效的插值方法；然而不同微波仪器的数据高度异质，给插值带来挑战。

Method: 提出一个生成模型，可处理跨样本变化、时间间隔不规则、地理错位、仪器特性不同的多源地理空间数据。采用自监督训练任务（随机掩码并重建一个源），并融合微波与红外数据。

Result: 自监督训练的CRPS显著低于监督训练；结合红外与微波数据优于仅用微波数据；生成模型的集合均值与确定性模型相当，但功率谱更接近真实观测。

Conclusion: 首次提出能够结合多个微波仪器和红外观测、在时间不规则条件下插值气旋微波图像的生成模型。

Abstract: Microwave satellite imagery plays a crucial role in monitoring tropical cyclone precipitation and intensity worldwide, but suffers from long revisit times, potentially missing rapid storm evolution phases. While this raises the need for an interpolation method, it is made challenging by the high level of heterogeneity of microwave data coming from different instruments. In this work, we introduce the first generative model that can be applied to multiple geospatial sources that change across samples, occur at irregular time intervals, are misaligned geographically, and come from instruments with varying characteristics. We apply this model to the case of spatio-temporal interpolation of tropical cyclone microwave images from other microwave and infrared instruments. We train using a self-supervised task in which a random source is masked and reconstructed, and show that it leads to a significant decrease in Continuous Ranked Probability Score over supervised training. We show a further improvement by combining infrared and microwave data compared to microwave only. Using these improvements, the generative model produces an ensemble mean on par with that of a deterministic model, while generating a power spectrum significantly closer to that of true observations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first generative model that interpolates microwave images of cyclones by combining multiple microwave instruments and infrared observations at irregular time intervals.

</details>


### [5] [Open-Vocabulary BEV Segmentation with 3D-Aware Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24353)
*Hojun Choi,Seulbin Hwang,Dae Jung Kim,Kisung Kim,Hyunjung Shim,Jinhan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OVBEVSeg，一种面向开放词汇鸟瞰图分割（OVBS）的几何感知框架，通过三阶段渐进式策略（2D到BEV伪标签、联合2D-BEV场景优化、3D几何蒸馏）解决2D视觉语言模型语义提升到BEV时的3D几何不一致问题，在nuScenes数据集上未见类别mIoU超出闭集方法15.3，推理速度提升2.5倍且显存仅为投影方法的0.22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV感知方法局限于闭集场景，无法处理真实世界中不可预见的类别；将2D VLM语义提升到BEV时存在严重的3D几何不一致性，导致开放词汇BEV分割面临挑战。

Method: 提出OVBEVSeg框架，包含三个渐进阶段：(1) 通过可靠3D投影生成2D到BEV的伪标签实现开放词汇泛化；(2) 联合2D-BEV逐场景优化，引入BEV结构约束保证3D几何一致性；(3) 3D几何蒸馏实现在线推理效率。基于高效高斯泼溅（GS）反投影技术。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，未见类别mIoU超出闭集方法15.3；无需新类标注即可与使用40%标注数据的自监督/半监督基线竞争；推理速度提升2.5倍，显存仅为投影方法的0.22倍。

Conclusion: OVBEVSeg首次实现了高效且几何一致的开放词汇BEV分割，在闭集、开放集和效率指标上均达到最优，为BEV感知从闭集走向开放世界提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bird's-eye view (BEV) perception fuses multi-camera images into a unified top-down representation for autonomous driving. Despite recent progress, state-of-the-art methods remain confined to closed-set scenarios, making them vulnerable to unpredictable real-world environments. In this work, we introduce open-vocabulary BEV segmentation (OVBS), which leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to recognize categories beyond the training set while maintaining precise BEV perception and real-time efficiency. A key challenge in OVBS lies in the 3D geometric inconsistency inherent in the ill-posed lifting of 2D VLM semantics into BEV. To address this, we propose OVBEVSeg, a geometry-aware OVBS framework that enhances efficient Gaussian splatting (GS)-based unprojection by leveraging robust 3D geometric constraints across three progressive stages: (1) 2D-to-BEV pseudo-labeling via reliable 3D projection for OV generalization; (2) joint 2D-BEV per-scene optimization with BEV structural constraints for 3D geometric consistency; and (3) 3D geometric distillation for online efficiency. On the nuScenes dataset, OVBEVSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming closed-set methods by 15.3 mIoU on unseen categories. Remarkably, even with no novel-class ground-truth labels, it remains competitive with self- and semi-supervised baselines trained with up to 40% of ground-truth annotations. Furthermore, it achieves 2.5x faster inference with only 0.22x the memory consumption of projection-based methods. Project page: https://hchoi256.github.io/projects/ovbevseg/.

</details>


### [6] [MedPCFM: Improving Medical Point Cloud Completion by Integrating Point Transformers and Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24433)
*Kamil Kwarciak,Marek Wodzinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCFM，一种基于PTv3的流匹配方法用于医学点云补全，在三个数据集上达到SOTA生成性能，相比扩散模型采样步数更少，速度提升最高7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学点云补全对于解剖结构重建和临床工作流至关重要，但该领域中的生成式建模研究尚不充分。

Method: 采用连续时间生成式建模，提出PCFM（PTv3支持的流匹配方法），并构建了确定性编码器-解码器基线以及扩散补全基线（PCDiff）作为对比。

Result: PCFM与确定性PTv3基线竞争力相当，在所有数据集上达到SOTA生成性能；采样步数远少于扩散模型；最优配置下PCFM相比PVCNN骨干网络实现最高7倍加速。

Conclusion: 流匹配方法在医学点云补全中生成性能优异且效率高，模型规模和点分辨率增加可带来一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Medical point cloud completion is important for anatomical reconstruction and downstream clinical workflows, yet generative modeling in this setting remains insufficiently studied. We investigate completion through continuous-time generative modeling and introduce PCFM, a PTv3-backed flow matching approach for medical point cloud completion. We evaluate on SkullFix and SkullBreak, and additionally on the more recent Mandibular Defect dataset. We build strong baselines by adapting PTv3 to a deterministic encoder-decoder completion model and by instantiating diffusion completion (PCDiff) with both PVCNN and PTv3 denoisers. PCFM with PTv3 is competitive with the deterministic PTv3 baseline and achieves state-of-the-art generative performance across datasets, while requiring substantially fewer sampling steps than diffusion. At the best operating points, PTv3 also yields clear throughput gains, providing up to a 7$\times$ speed-up for PCFM compared to a PVCNN backbone. Finally, we study empirical scaling trends by varying model size and point cardinality, showing consistent gains with higher point resolution and informative trade-offs across model scales.

</details>


### [7] [EERLoss: A Novel Loss Function for Training Deep Biometric Models. A Case Study in Keystroke Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24586)
*Nahuel Gonzalez,Marta Robledo-Moreno,Ivan DeAndres-Tame,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Ruben Tolosana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EERLoss，一种可微的、能近似等错误率（EER）的损失函数，用于直接优化生物特征验证模型。在超18.5万人的大规模击键动态验证基准KVC-onGoing上，相比现有最先进方法，EERLoss收敛更快，且将EER相对降低约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习生物特征验证模型通常优化间接目标函数，与主要评估指标等错误率（EER）之间存在不对齐问题，导致优化过程与评价指标脱节。

Method: 提出EERLoss，一种次可微的、可任意精度逼近EER的损失函数，并具有可扩展性以优化DET曲线上任意特定工作点。

Result: 在KVC-onGoing基准上（18.5万+受试者），全面消融实验表明EERLoss优于现有损失函数，收敛速度显著更快；用EERLoss重新训练KVC-winning架构，EER相对降低约30%。

Conclusion: EERLoss作为一种任务对齐的训练目标，特别适合高变异性的生物特征（如击键动态），在大规模挑战性基准上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Deep learning approaches to biometric verification are commonly trained by optimizing indirect objectives, creating a misalignment between the optimization process and the primary evaluation metric, typically the Equal Error Rate (EER). This paper introduces EERLoss: a subdifferentiable, arbitrarily accurate approximation to EER for training deep biometric models. Furthermore, this framework has the potential to be adapted to optimize any specific operating point on the DET curve, enhancing its generalizability. To validate this approach, EERLoss is evaluated on a particularly demanding behavioral biometric modality: keystroke dynamics verification. This task is characterized by its high intra-class and low inter-class variability. Experiments are conducted on the large-scale KVC-onGoing benchmark, incorporating data from over 185,000 subjects across different scenarios. A comprehensive ablation study initially demonstrates the superiority of EERLoss in comparison to existing state-of-the-art loss functions. It also converges substantially faster compared to other losses, reducing the overall training cost. Additionally, a comparison is made between the proposed loss and the KVC-winning architecture by re-training it with EERLoss, demonstrating that the proposed approach significantly outperforms the original SoTA, achieving a relative EER reduction of up to approx. 30\%. This improvement on a challenging, large-scale benchmark validates the effectiveness of EERLoss as a task-aligned training objective specifically suited for high-variance biometric traits.

</details>

### [8] [Self-Recognition Finetuning can Prevent and Reverse Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23700)
*Arush Tagade,Shaoheng Zhou,Jiaxin Wen,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现对齐破坏（EM）并非模型学习有害内容，而是对模型已对齐角色的破坏。通过自生成文本识别（SGTR）微调作为面向角色的干预手段，实验证明SGTR在预防EM方面效果显著，而所有干预在逆转EM时效果相当，仅能恢复被EM削弱的能力。进一步证据表明EM与LLM默认角色密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现EM与激活失调人格向量和邪恶特征相关，暗示EM通过破坏模型已对齐的角色而非直接学习有害内容起作用。基于此，研究者希望探索一种面向角色的干预方法，并进一步验证EM的本质是角色不稳定化而非采纳失调人格。

Method: 采用两阶段微调实验，在GPT-4.1、Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct、Seed-OSS-36B-Instruct三个模型上使用多个EM数据集，对比SGTR微调与良性微调基线（正确领域特定数据、通用知识、单词计数）在逆转和预防两种设置下的效果。

Result: 所有干预在逆转EM时效果相当，但仅在恢复EM已削弱的能力时有效。在预防设置中，仅SGTR微调能持续降低失调，且不加剧任何单一指标。此外，EM微调使LLM身份自我报告多样性增加，人工破坏自我识别会加剧EM，移除身份承载系统提示可大幅降低EM效果。

Conclusion: EM不是模型采纳一致的失调人格，而是对已对齐角色的不稳定化。SGTR微调作为角色强化手段在预防EM方面具有独特优势。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) has been linked to the activation of misaligned persona vectors and evil character traits, suggesting that EM operates through disruption of the model's aligned character rather than direct learning of harmful content. Motivated by this connection, we study self-generated text recognition (SGTR) finetuning as a character-targeted intervention that is distinct from existing in-training defenses. We conduct two-stage finetuning experiments across three models (GPT-4.1, Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct, Seed-OSS-36B-Instruct) and multiple EM datasets to compare SGTR finetuning against benign finetuning baselines (correct domain-specific data, general knowledge, and word counting) to find it an effective defense in both reversal and prevention settings. We find that all interventions produce comparable EM reversal, but only when restoring capabilities that EM had degraded. For prevention, only SGTR finetuning consistently reduces misalignment without exacerbating any individual metric, suggesting that character fortification specifically drives prevention. We provide further evidence for EM's relation to the LLM's default character by showing that EM finetuning induces diversity into the LLM's identity self-reports, artificially corrupting self-recognition exacerbates misalignment caused by EM finetuning, and that removing the model's identity-bearing system prompt substantially reduces the effect of EM finetuning. Together, these findings reframe EM not as the adoption of a coherent misaligned persona but as the destabilization of aligned character.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluating LLM Usage for Efficient and Explainable Numerical and Classified Implicit Sentiment Analysis of Product Desirability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23701)
*Sherri Weitl-Harms,John Hastings*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展且可解释的框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）对定性产品反馈进行零样本情感评分与分类，在两个PDT数据集上达到最高0.97的皮尔逊相关性和94%的分类准确率，且GPT-4o-mini以94%更低的成本实现了与大型模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定性产品反馈蕴含细微的用户体验信息，但其隐含情感难以量化衡量。现有方法缺乏可扩展性和可解释性，亟需一种既能准确量化情感、又能提供透明可解释结果的框架。

Method: 采用零样本方法，利用LLMs直接对定性文本进行连续数值情感评分和分类情感分类，无需预训练的评论评分数据。框架还整合了模型置信度评分和人类可读的推理解释（xAI），并在ZORQ和CARMA两个PDT数据集上，与词典基线方法和Transformer基线方法进行了对比评估。

Result: LLMs生成的数值情感评分与专家标签高度一致，皮尔逊相关性最高达0.97，分类准确率最高达94%。LLMs对多种数据形式表现稳健，置信度评分一致较高。词典和Transformer基线方法未产生统计显著结果。GPT-4o-mini以94%更低成本达到与大型模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: 结合PDT调查方法与低成本LLM的情感分析框架，能够提供丰富的数值和分类情感评分以及高层用户印象，可用于产品开发改进和营销定位，同时通过置信度评分和xAI推理增强了可解释性、透明度和信任度。

Abstract: Qualitative product feedback can reveal nuanced user experiences, but its implicit sentiment is difficult to measure. This paper presents a scalable and interpretable framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to quantify product desirability from such data. Using two Product Desirability Toolkit (PDT) datasets from ZORQ and CARMA comprising 106 respondent term groupings with gold-standard human annotation, zero-shot continuous numerical sentiment scoring and categorical sentiment classification are evaluated without relying on explicit review scores. Across the datasets, LLMs generated numerical sentiment scores directly from qualitative responses and closely matched expert labels, achieving Pearson correlations up to 0.97 and classification accuracy up to 94%. LLMs maintained robustness even when handling data presented in multiple forms and consistently expressed high confidence. In contrast, lexicon-based and transformer baselines did not produce statistically significant results. Among the models tested, GPT-4o-mini achieved performance comparable to larger models at 94% lower cost, supporting scalable deployment.
  The framework also incorporates model confidence ratings and human-readable rationale explanations (xAI), improving interpretability, transparency, and trust while supporting practical use in product satisfaction assessment. In general, using the PDT tool as a survey method along with a cost efficient LLM for sentiment analysis has the potential to provide for product evaluation with results that are rich in terms of sentiment scores (both numerical and classified sentiment) and in terms of the high-level user impressions of the product that can be used to identify ideas for product development and improvement, as well as marketing ideas for target audiences.

</details>


### [10] [Do LLM Attribution Metrics Transfer? Auditing Retrieval-Augmented Generation Evaluation Across Datasets and Constructs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23915)
*Tianyu Ding,Aditya Nannapaneni,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了8种自动评分器在3种检索增强生成归因评估体系中的跨数据集迁移能力，发现在生成答案归因方面（最大数据集覆盖），没有任何评分器能在所有数据集上保持一致表现；评分器排名在不同数据集间出现反转（如NLI评分器在短文本数据集上AUROC达0.90，在长文本数据集上跌至0.53），导致"最佳平均"选策策略失效（留一法平均遗憾0.172 AUROC）。基于提示的LLM裁判虽避免了随机水平的崩溃，但成本高约100倍且非确定性，并未消除验证负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前实践中常将自动归因评估指标视为可互换使用，但缺乏系统验证其跨数据集一致性的研究。本研究旨在检验这些评分器在不同评估体系与数据集间的可迁移性，揭示其潜在的不稳定性及其决策成本。

Method: 审计8种自动评分器（包括词汇、嵌入、BERTScore基线以及基于蕴涵/基础训练的模型），在3种评估构建（出处/主题性、生成答案归因、事实验证蕴含）上，通过95%置信区间检验评分器是否能在多数据集构建的每个数据集中保持最优表现；使用AttributionBench（4个数据集，n=1,610）和独立HAGRID（n=2,150）进行跨数据集分析，并比较基于提示的LLM裁判。

Result: 在生成答案归因构建中，无任何评分器能在所有数据集上保持在最优评分器95%置信区间内；评分器排名在数据集间出现显著反转（Kendall tau=-0.64, p=0.031），NLI评分器在AttributedQA上AUROC为0.90，在LFQA上跌至0.53（随机水平），而BERTScore在LFQA上达0.91；非长度或截断伪影所致。"最佳平均"选策策略留一法平均遗憾0.172 AUROC，劣于固定单一评分器；LLM裁判避免了随机崩溃但非最优，成本高约100倍且结果非确定性。

Conclusion: 自动归因评分器在不同数据集间缺乏可迁移性，评分器选择必须在目标数据集上验证而非从其他数据集学习；LLM裁判虽解决了部分问题但引入新的成本与不确定性，验证负担只是转移而非消除。

Abstract: Practice often treats automatic metrics for attribution in LLM retrieval-augmented generation as interchangeable. We audit eight automatic scorers -- lexical, embedding, and BERTScore baselines alongside entailment/grounding-trained models (clean and FEVER NLI, the checker MiniCheck) -- across three evaluation constructs (provenance/topicality, generated-answer attribution, and fact-check entailment), asking whether any scorer transfers: stays within the 95% confidence interval of the best audited scorer on every dataset of a multi-dataset construct. In the construct with the most multi-dataset human-labeled coverage -- generated-answer attribution (AttributionBench's four source datasets, n = 1,610, with independent HAGRID, n = 2,150) -- none does: the per-dataset metric rankings invert (Kendall tau = -0.64, p = 0.031 on AttributedQA vs. LFQA), and an off-the-shelf NLI scorer that is best on short-claim AttributedQA (AUROC 0.90) collapses to AUROC 0.53 (chance) on long-form LFQA, where BERTScore wins (0.91); the flip is not a length or truncation artifact. This instability has a concrete decision cost: a naive "best-on-average" rule for choosing an evaluator fails leave-one-dataset-out (mean held-out regret 0.172 AUROC, worse than fixing one scorer), so metric choice must be validated on the target dataset rather than learned from others. A prompt-based LLM judge avoids the chance-level collapses the automatic scorers suffer (no LFQA collapse) but is not uniformly best, ~100x costlier, and non-deterministic -- relocating, not removing, the validation burden.

</details>


### [11] [When Retrieval Metrics Mislead: Measuring Policy Signal in Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23937)
*Tianyu Ding,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在tau-bench环境下的策略分类任务中，精确匹配检索召回率是否能够准确反映检索器为下游决策模型提供的策略上下文效用。实验发现，虽然精确匹配召回率很低（仅7%），但使用检索到的策略条款进行分类的宏F1分数（0.58）与使用真实条款（0.60）几乎相当，远高于无策略基线（0.21）和错误策略基线（0.32），表明精确匹配召回率可能低估检索器的下游效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确匹配检索召回率常被用作检索器是否为下游决策模型提供有用策略上下文的代理指标，但该代理指标的有效性尚未得到充分验证。本文旨在通过端到端的分类实验检验这一假设。

Method: 在tau-bench环境中，使用Qwen2.5-3B/7B分类器进行预行动策略分类。首先在黄金策略条件下对比紧凑结构化状态与原始轨迹的性能差异；然后将基准指定的策略条款替换为从决策上下文检索到的排序最高的条款，比较检索条款与黄金条款的分类性能，并设置不匹配策略和无策略控制组。

Result: 黄金策略条件下，紧凑结构化状态比原始轨迹的宏F1提升0.13-0.17。虽然精确匹配条款仅7%排首位，但3B分类器使用检索条款的宏F1为0.58，黄金条款为0.60（差值-0.02，95% CI [-0.23,+0.21]）；不匹配策略和无策略控制组分别为0.32和0.21。未检测到检索条款与黄金条款间的显著宏F1差异，但置信区间较宽，无法建立非劣效性。

Conclusion: 在该基准设定下，精确匹配条款召回率可能低估下游策略效用，建议在分类循环中使用检索到的策略进行评估，而非仅依靠召回率指标。

Abstract: Exact-match retrieval recall is often used as a proxy for whether a retriever supplies useful policy context to a downstream decision model. We test this proxy for pre-action policy classification in tau-bench using Qwen2.5-3B/7B classifiers. Under gold-policy conditioning, a compact structured state improves macro-F1 over raw trajectories by 0.13-0.17 after tuning. We then replace the benchmark-designated policy clause with the top-ranked clause retrieved from decision-time context. Although the exact governing clause is retrieved at rank 1 for only 7% of airline states, the primary 3B classifier obtains macro-F1 0.58 with retrieved clauses versus 0.60 with gold clauses (Delta=-0.02, task-cluster 95% CI [-0.23,+0.21]); mismatched-policy and no-policy controls score 0.32 and 0.21. We do not detect a macro-F1 difference between retrieved and gold clauses in this configuration, although the interval remains too wide to establish non-inferiority. The same qualitative pattern appears with a second retriever and at 7B, while varying across fine-tuning configurations. These results indicate that exact-match clause recall can underestimate downstream policy utility in this benchmark setting, motivating evaluation with retrieved policies in the classification loop rather than recall alone.

</details>


### [12] [Does My Embedding Reflect That $A = B$? Evaluating Mathematical Equivalence in Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23959)
*Jiaying Ye,Samarth Rao,Leo Carlin,Kedar Chintalapati,Saharsh Bhargava,Rachit Jaiswal,Michael Zhou,Jared Darlington,Jarod Alper,Vasily Ilin,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现当前嵌入模型将数学语句按术语而非数学本质分组，为此构建了MELD数据集（数学等价但词汇不同），并提出一种对比学习方法，通过对齐非正式语句与不同形式化表示来改进嵌入，在非正式-形式检索和纯自然语言MELD任务上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学的抽象性导致同一命题在不同子领域有截然不同的表述形式，许多突破源于研究者发现某个问题在其他领域已有解答。同时，形式化资源（如Lean到自然语言）的增长需要能够高效可靠地在数学"语言"间导航的工具。现有嵌入模型能否捕捉数学等价性尚不清楚，亟需评估和改进。

Method: 构建MELD数据集（Mathematically Equivalent but Lexically Different Pairs），收集数学等价但词汇差异大的语句对；提出对比学习框架，对齐非正式语句与不同形式化表示以训练嵌入模型。

Result: 当前最先进的嵌入模型倾向于按使用的术语而非底层数学含义对语句进行分组；所提出的对比方法不仅在非正式-形式检索任务上表现出色，在仅包含自然语言语句的MELD任务上也取得了改进。

Conclusion: 现有嵌入模型未能捕捉数学等价性，所提出的对比对齐方法能有效改善这一问题，实现跨数学"语言"的数学等价识别。

Abstract: Because mathematics is highly abstract, a single statement can take very different forms depending on what subfield it is framed in. There are many examples where breakthroughs occurred after researchers discovered that a question had already been answered in a different field. At the same time, the growth of new resources related to formalization has increased the need for tools that enable efficient and reliable navigation between mathematical 'languages' (e.g., from Lean to natural language). In this paper, we investigate whether current embedding models capture mathematical equivalence. To do this, we introduce the Mathematically Equivalent but Lexically Different Pairs (MELD) Dataset, a collection of mathematically equivalent statements that are expressed in very different language. We show that current state-of-the-art embedding models tend to group statements by the terminology used to make them instead of the underlying math. Motivated by this, we propose a contrastive approach to learning embeddings of mathematical text that focuses on aligning informal statements with different formalizations. Our experiments demonstrate that this leads to improvements not only on informal-formal retrieval tasks but also on MELD, which only contains natural language statements.

</details>


### [13] [RASC+: Retrieval-Constrained LLM Adjudication for Clinical Value Set Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23992)
*Sumit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分阶段的值集完成方法：先通过Qwen3检索器优化候选池构建（召回率从0.553提升至0.730），再使用GPT-5作为约束条件下的LLM裁决器进行候选选择，在RASC基准上将宏F1从0.287提升至0.549，同时保证所有返回代码均来自可审计的候选池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床值集在质量测量、表型分析、队列构建和临床决策支持中至关重要，但直接的零样本大语言模型生成不适合此任务，因为临床代码系统规模大、有版本控制且难以被语言模型可靠记忆。

Method: 分阶段方法：第一阶段使用Qwen3检索器结合词汇感知扩展和代码显示救援检索优化候选池构建（追求高召回率）；第二阶段使用GPT-5作为约束条件下的LLM裁决器进行候选选择（追求精确选择），所有返回代码必须来自可审计的候选池。

Result: 在RASC基准上，候选池召回率从0.553提升至0.730；使用GPT-5裁决后，全测试宏F1从0.287提升至0.549，持有出版商宏F1从0.233提升至0.533。

Conclusion: 检索约束下的LLM裁决能够显著改进值集完成性能，同时保持所有返回代码来自可审计候选池的安全约束。

Abstract: Clinical value sets define the standardized terminology codes used in quality measurement, phenotyping, cohort construction, and clinical decision support. The recently introduced Retrieval-Augmented Set Completion (RASC) benchmark showed that direct zero-shot large language model (LLM) generation is poorly suited to this task: clinical code systems are large, version-controlled, and not reliably memorized by language models. We study a stage-wise alternative in which candidate-pool construction is optimized for recall and a constrained LLM adjudicator is optimized for candidate selection. On the full 3,744-value-set RASC test split, Qwen3-based retrieval with vocabulary-aware expansion and code-display rescue retrieval increases candidate-pool recall from the original RASC retrieval baseline of 0.553 to 0.730; on the held-out-publisher stratum, pool recall is 0.655. The higher-recall pool alone is not sufficient: applying the original SAPBert cross-encoder to this expanded pool gives full-test macro F1 of 0.287 and held-out-publisher macro F1 of 0.233. Replacing the stage-2 selector with blinded GPT-5 adjudication over the same pool increases full-test macro F1 to 0.549 and held-out-publisher macro F1 to 0.533. These results show that retrieval-constrained LLM adjudication can substantially improve value set completion while preserving the safety constraint that all returned codes must come from an auditable candidate pool.

</details>


### [14] [CAVEWOMAN: How Large Language Models Behave Under Linguistic Input and Output Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24083)
*Morayo Danielle Adeyemi,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Cavewoman是一个双通道评估协议，用于衡量用户提示压缩和模型回复压缩对任务准确率、实际成本和文本一致性的影响。实验发现：输出压缩可降低实际成本（1.4-3x），而输入压缩反而增加成本（~1.15x，最高2.7x）并损害准确性，且生成文本与无约束基线产生显著分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前广泛推崇通过“缩短文本、丢弃语法、节省token”的原始风格（caveman style）来降低推理成本，但压缩实际效果取决于压缩的是用户提示通道还是模型回复通道，缺乏统一的评估协议来系统衡量双通道压缩的影响。

Method: 提出Cavewoman双通道评估协议：对每次生成同时度量任务准确率、实际每项成本和参考文本一致性（与模型无约束基线对比）。在5个数据集上对8个模型（含API模型和开放权重模型）进行5个压缩水平的全面评估，双通道在同一项上测量。

Result: 输出压缩：对多数API模型降低实际成本1.4-2.4x（最优可达3x），对所有开放权重模型在公共定价下也降低成本。输入压缩：反而增加净成本（5基准平均~1.15x，最差数据集1.8x，强压缩下2.7x），因为模型用更长回复补偿，同时准确性崩溃。表面文本与无约束基线显著分歧：非推理模型中约一半正确生成的文本不再蕴含基线生成。该分歧经长度控制重评分、多重比较校正和互补语义度量复现后依然成立。

Conclusion: 输出压缩是降低推理成本的有效策略，而输入压缩是严格的“双输”策略：既增加成本又损害准确性和文本一致性。研究提供了两个通道压缩效果的全面实证，为实际部署中的成本优化策略选择提供了定量依据。

Abstract: "Talk short. Drop grammar. Save token." This caveman style is widely promoted as a way to cut inference cost, but whether it actually saves anything depends on which channel (the user's prompt or the model's response) is being compressed. We present Cavewoman, a two-channel evaluation protocol that scores every generation on task accuracy, realized per-item cost, and reference-text agreement against the model's unconstrained reference. We evaluate eight models on five datasets at five reduction levels, with both channels measured on the same items. Output compression cuts realized cost on most API models (1.4-2.4x per model, up to 3x in the best case) and on all four open-weight models under public-tier pricing. Input compression has the opposite effect, a strict lose-lose: it raises net cost rather than lowering it (~1.15x on the five-benchmark mean, up to 1.8x on the worst dataset and 2.7x under stronger compression), because models compensate with longer responses even as accuracy collapses. Under the same setting, surface text diverges from the unconstrained reference: on the non-reasoning models, roughly half of all generations are correct yet their surface text no longer entails the model's own unconstrained baseline generation. The divergence survives length-controlled re-scoring, multiple-comparisons correction, and replication under complementary semantic measures. Code and data are available at https://github.com/danielle34/cavewoman.

</details>


### [15] [PORTER: Language-Grounded Event Representations for Portable Structured EHR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24102)
*Lin Lawrence Guo,Adam Paul Yan,Emily Vettese,Lillian Sung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PORTER是一种语言基础的电子健康记录（EHR）基础模型，通过将事件表示与固定词表解耦，利用冻结文本编码器和数值处理通路，实现了跨词汇表的无缝迁移，在74项临床预测任务中匹配了固定词表模型的性能，并在跨词汇表迁移中恢复了97.1%的AUROC，且计算成本降低329倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR基础模型将临床事件编码为固定词汇表中的离散事件令牌，因此无法直接表示包含未见概念或新属性组合（如数值）的事件，这限制了模型在不同机构甚至同一机构内不同部署流程间的迁移能力。

Method: PORTER通过以下方式实现词汇无关的EHR建模：(1) 使用冻结文本编码器将事件描述转换为表示；(2) 通过专用通路集成数值信息；(3) 采用自回归预训练的时间骨干网络学习患者时间线上的临床动态。

Result: 在儿科医院的74项临床预测任务中，PORTER与具有相同时间骨干网络和预训练目标的固定词表模型平均AUROC相当。当使用预训练中未见的事件描述时，PORTER无需重新训练或词表映射，恢复了直接在目标词表上训练模型平均AUROC的97.1%。迁移到MIMIC数据集时，PORTER优于固定词表模型（后者因69%事件令牌未见而失效）。数值通路增强了对数值大小的敏感性，且不干扰临床概念身份。PORTER的AUROC高于任务特定的文本序列化比较器，计算成本降低329倍。

Conclusion: PORTER向词汇无关的EHR基础模型迈出了一步，减少了词汇统一的需求，同时保持了领域内性能并实现了跨任务的高效复用。

Abstract: Most electronic health record (EHR) foundation models encode clinical events as discrete event tokens from a fixed vocabulary and therefore cannot directly represent events containing unseen concepts or new combinations of concepts and attributes such as numeric values. This limits transfer across institutions and even across deployment pipelines within the same institution. We introduce PORTER, a language-grounded structured EHR foundation model that decouples event representation from this fixed vocabulary. PORTER represents events through their descriptions using a frozen text encoder, integrates numeric values through a dedicated pathway, and learns clinical dynamics over patient timelines with an autoregressively pretrained temporal backbone. Across 74 clinical prediction tasks at a pediatric hospital, PORTER matched the mean AUROC of a fixed-vocabulary model with the same temporal backbone and pretraining objective. When the same patient timelines were rendered using event descriptions not seen during pretraining, PORTER transferred without retraining or vocabulary mapping, recovering 97.1% of the mean AUROC of a model trained directly on the target vocabulary. When transferred to MIMIC, PORTER outperformed the fixed-vocabulary model, which dropped 69% of events because their tokens were unseen. Mechanistic analyses showed cross-vocabulary transfer tracked preservation of patient-level representation geometry rather than the scale of the text encoder, and the numeric pathway improved sensitivity to magnitude without disrupting clinical concept identity. PORTER also achieved higher AUROC than a task-specific text serialization comparator, at 329-fold lower amortized compute. PORTER is a step toward vocabulary-independent EHR foundation models that reduce the need for vocabulary harmonization while preserving in-domain performance and enabling efficient cross-task reuse.

</details>


### [16] [BehaviorBench: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Behavioral Science Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24162)
*Jin Huang,Yutong Xie,Wanli Song,Xingjian Zhang,Walter Yuan,Matthew O. Jackson,Qiaozhu Mei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BehaviorBench基准，系统评估基础模型在行为科学四大核心能力上的表现，并开发了Be.FM-1.5模型。结果表明：通用模型在个体预测和知识任务上占优，而行为基础模型在分布对齐上更强；Be.FM-1.5在分布指标上领先、个体指标上具有竞争力，证明适当的行为适应可以弥合差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在行为科学领域（心理学、社会学、经济学）的应用日益增多，但在多样化的行为科学任务、情境和人群中的系统表现尚缺乏全面理解。

Method: 构建BehaviorBench综合基准，评估基础模型在四个核心能力（行为预测与模拟、战略决策、主体特质推断、行为知识应用）上的表现，在个体和分布两个层级进行评测；并基于该基准训练Be.FM-1.5模型。

Result: 通用模型在个体级预测和知识密集型任务上表现优异，而行为基础模型在分布对齐上显著更强；Be.FM-1.5在分布指标上领先，在个体指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 分布级评测至关重要；BehaviorBench可作为开发与评估行为对齐AI系统的基础；适当的行为适应（如Be.FM-1.5）能弥合通用模型与行为专门模型之间的差距。

Abstract: Foundation models have been increasingly applied to behavioral science domains such as psychology, sociology, and economics. While these models show promise in individual tasks such as survey response prediction and human-subject experiment simulation, there remains no systematic understanding of how well they perform across diverse behavioral science tasks, contexts, and populations. We introduce BehaviorBench, a comprehensive benchmark that evaluates foundation models along four core capabilities: (1) behavior prediction and simulation, (2) strategic decision-making, (3) subject-trait inference, and (4) behavioral knowledge application. Crucially, BehaviorBench evaluates model outputs at both the individual and distributional levels, capturing not only per-subject accuracy but also population-level alignment, an essential requirement for behavioral validity. Leveraging the tasks in BehaviorBench, we further develop Be.FM-1.5, extending the Be.FM family of behavioral foundation models fine-tuned on behavioral data. Our results reveal a considerable gap: proprietary general-purpose models excel at individual-level prediction and knowledge-intensive tasks, whereas behavioral foundation models, fine-tuned on behavioral data, achieve substantially stronger distributional alignment. Notably, Be.FM-1.5 leads on distributional metrics and remains competitive on individual-level metrics, suggesting that proper behavioral adaptation can close the gap. Our results highlight the importance of distributional evaluation, establish BehaviorBench as a foundation for developing and assessing behaviorally aligned AI systems, and demonstrate Be.FM-1.5's potential for a broad range of behavioral science studies. Our BehaviorBench and Be.FM-1.5 models can be accessed via https://umich-foreseer.github.io/behaviorbench/.

</details>


### [17] [A Pāninian Foundation for Indic Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24172)
*Ritwik Banerjee,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于波你尼语法框架（Astādhyāyī）的统一计算架构，以解决印度语言自然语言处理基础设施碎片化问题，并设计了一套四部分基准测试套件来显式化这种共享的形态句法结构，同时探讨神经网络模型是否能自发学习波你尼语法范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超过十亿人使用的印度语言在自然语言处理领域面临基础设施碎片化和发展不足的结构性问题，因为现有方法按单个语言或小语系组织工具和基准，忽视了印度语言通过两千多年围绕梵语趋同而形成的共享形态句法架构。

Method: 提出基于波你尼《Astādhyāyī》语法框架的统一计算架构，并设计包含四部分的基准测试套件，将跨语言的共享形态句法结构显式化、可度量化，以便应用于实际系统。

Result: 该框架有望提升印度语言系统的准确性、数据效率和可迁移性，将分散的稀疏印度语言资源融合为单一的高资源元语言基础。

Conclusion: 波你尼语法框架为印度语言NLP提供了缺失的统一计算架构，同时为可解释性研究提出关键问题：神经模型是否能自主习得波你尼的语法范畴。

Abstract: More than a billion people communicate in Indic languages, yet the natural language processing infrastructure serving them remains fragmented and underdeveloped. The cause is structural: the field organizes its tools and benchmarks around individual languages or small subsets of genealogical language families, building separate analyzers, parsers, and datasets for each language and starting over for the next. This overlooks a deep regularity. Through more than two millennia of convergence around Sanskrit, Indic languages came to share a morphosyntactic architecture formalized in Pānini's grammar, the Astādhyāyī. This cuts across genealogical lines, uniting languages through a common framework. We argue that this Pāninian framework supplies a unifying computational architecture the field has lacked, and that benchmarks grounded explicitly in it would make Indic language systems more accurate, more data-efficient, and more transferable, effectively merging many apparently disparate and sparse Indic language resources into a single high-resource metalanguage bedrock. We propose a four-part benchmark suite to render this shared architecture explicit, measurable, and ready to be leveraged for practical applications. Moreover, we underscore the question it raises for interpretability research: whether neural models trained on these languages come to represent Pānini's categories on their own.

</details>


### [18] [Harmonic: Hierarchical State Space Models for Efficient Long-Context Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24650)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Harmonic是一种分层状态空间模型，通过堆叠三个不同时间尺度的循环层级，并传递预测误差而非原始隐藏状态，在语言建模中显著优于Transformer和Mamba，尤其在长序列上表现突出，且计算复杂度为O(L)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer在长序列上存在二次复杂度问题，且依赖于位置编码（如RoPE）导致序列长度受限；Mamba等SSM虽改进了效率，但在极长序列上仍存在内存瓶颈。作者旨在设计一种可扩展至超长序列的高效语言模型架构。

Method: 提出Harmonic架构，由三个递归层级堆叠而成，每个层级以不同时间尺度运行，且下层将预测误差（而非隐藏状态）传递给上层。这种设计允许模型在不同粒度上捕捉序列依赖关系，同时保持线性复杂度O(L)。

Result: 在enwiki8上，Harmonic在1K/8K/32K token长度下分别以+1.4%/+6.7%/+11.4%的bpt优势超越同等规模的Transformer（28M参数），并以0.7-1.8%的优势超越Mamba；在64K长度上，Mamba和Transformer均显存溢出，Harmonic仍成功训练并达到6.169 bpt。在WikiText-103上复现了类似结果。在1B参数规模下，用HarmonicBlock替换TinyLlama的所有注意力层后，模型在1K-8K长度上保持稳定loss，而原始TinyLlama在超出2K RoPE限制后性能急剧下降（Lambada上8K长度差距达+9.4 bpt）。

Conclusion: Harmonic通过层级预测误差传递机制，有效克服了Transformer的长序列二次复杂度和RoPE长度限制问题，同时具备比Mamba更好的长序列可扩展性，为构建高效长上下文语言模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present Harmonic, a hierarchical state space model (SSM) for language modeling. The architecture stacks three recurrent levels at progressively slower timescales; each level receives the prediction error of the level below as input, rather than its raw hidden state. On enwiki8 with equal token budgets, Harmonic outperforms a comparable Transformer (28M params) by +1.4% at 1K tokens, +6.7% at 8K tokens, and +11.4% at 32K tokens (bpt, lower is better). It also outperforms Mamba at every tested length by 0.7--1.8%. At 64K tokens, both Mamba and Transformer run out of memory on an 80GB H100; Harmonic trains successfully, reaching 6.169 bpt. Results replicate on WikiText-103 (H-TF gap +1.7% to +7.2% across 1K--32K). At 1B parameter scale, replacing all attention layers in TinyLlama 1.1B with HarmonicBlock eliminates the RoPE positional encoding limit: the resulting Hallamonic model maintains stable loss across sequence lengths 1K--8K on two independent clean benchmarks (Lambada and fineweb-edu held-out), while TinyLlama degrades catastrophically past its 2K-token RoPE limit (gap: +9.4 bpt at seq=8K on Lambada). Compute is O(L) per forward pass vs. O(L^2) for attention.
  Logs: https://github.com/Omibranch/harmonic-logs.

</details>


### [19] [AI-PAVE-Br: Leveraging Large Language Models for Enhanced Product Attribute Value Extraction through a Golden Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24655)
*Murilo Gazzola,Hugo Gobato Souto,Samuel Silva,Júlia Schubert Peixoto,Felipe Siqueira,André Luis Pedroso de Morais,Caio Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对巴西电商领域产品属性值提取（PAVE）任务，提出了基于大语言模型的AI-PAVEBr系统，并构建了首个高质量葡语PAVE基准数据集Golden Set。实验证明，通过目标提示工程，该系统在准确率上显著超越传统NER方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西电商产品数据爆炸式增长且高度复杂，传统NER方法难以处理葡萄牙语的多样性和语言细微差别，亟需专门化的高精度PAVE解决方案。

Method: 1) 构建AI-PAVEBr系统，基于大语言模型（LLMs）并采用目标提示工程（targeted prompt engineering）；2) 创建并公开Golden Set数据集，包含实体（Entity）、类别（Category）、子类别（Subcategories）三层结构的精细人工标注。3) 将AI-PAVEBr与传统NER基线进行对比实验。

Result: AI-PAVEBr在葡萄牙语电商产品属性提取任务上，准确率显著优于传统NER基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为非英语主要市场（巴西）提供了可扩展的高性能PAVE方案，并为NLP社区贡献了首个公开可用的葡语PAVE基准资源，促进了该方向的可重复研究。

Abstract: The explosive growth and complexity of product data within the dynamic Brazilian e-commerce landscape demand robust and specialized methods for structured information extraction. Traditional approaches to Product Attribute Value Extraction (PAVE) often struggle with the linguistic nuances and sheer diversity of product descriptions in Portuguese. To address this critical gap, this paper introduces two major contributions. First, we present AI-PAVEBr, a specialized system engineered with Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform high-accuracy PAVE specifically for Brazilian e-commerce catalogs. Second, to facilitate reproducible research and provide a definitive benchmark, we introduce and share the Golden Set, a new, meticulously curated, and manually annotated dataset for PAVE in Portuguese. We detail the creation process and structure (Entity, Category, Subcategories) of this high-quality reference set. Our experiments conclusively show that AI-PAVE-Br, leveraging targeted prompt engineering, dramatically outperforms conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) baselines. This work not only delivers a superior, scalable solution for a major non-English market but also enriches the NLP community with a valuable, publicly available resource for future PAVE research.

</details>


### [20] [L3Cube-MahaPOS: A Marathi Part-of-Speech Tagging Dataset and BERT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24825)
*Hariom Ingle,Ronit Ghode,Ishwari Gondkar,Jidnyasa Harad,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了L3Cube-MahaPOS，一个包含32,354条人工标注句子的马拉地语词性标注黄金标准数据集，基于16标签UD对齐方案，并评测了六类模型，最优系统达到88.67%的token级准确率和81.67%的宏F1值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马拉地语虽有超过8300万使用人口，位列全球前二十大语言，但在标注语料库和标准化评测基准方面严重资源匮乏，且其丰富的形态、自由语序、无大写约定以及与印地语/英语的语码混合给计算建模带来独特挑战，亟需高质量的POS标注数据集。

Method: 人工构建：从新闻文本中收集32,354条句子，由马拉地语熟练的标注团队按照16标签的Universal Dependencies对齐方案完全人工标注；经过Unicode标准化、天城文感知的分词和噪声过滤等预处理流程；评测模型涵盖HMM、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM+CharCNN、MuRIL和马拉地语专用Transformer MahaBERT-v2。

Result: 最佳系统（MahaBERT-v2）达到88.67%的token级准确率和81.67%的宏F1值（15个标注类别）。

Conclusion: 发布了数据集、标注指南和训练好的模型检查点，以促进马拉地语NLP的进一步研究。

Abstract: Part-of-Speech (POS) tagging is a foundational NLP task underpinning machine translation, information extraction, and syntactic parsing. Despite Marathi being spoken by over 83 million people and ranking among the top twenty most spoken languages worldwide, it remains severely under-resourced in annotated corpora and standardised evaluation benchmarks. Marathi presents unique challenges for computational modelling owing to its rich morphology, relatively free word order, lack of capitalisation conventions, and pervasive code-mixing with Hindi and English. We introduce L3Cube-MahaPOS, a gold-standard POS tagging dataset for Marathi comprising 32,354 manually annotated sentences drawn from news text. Annotation was performed entirely manually by a team of Marathi-proficient annotators following a 16-tag Universal Dependencies-aligned scheme. A structured preprocessing pipeline covering Unicode normalisation, Devanagari-aware tokenisation, and noise filtering ensures label consistency across all splits. We benchmark the dataset across six model families spanning HMM, CRF, BiLSTM, BiLSTM+CharCNN, MuRIL, and the Marathi-specific transformer MahaBERT-v2. The best system achieves 88.67\% token-level accuracy and a macro-F1 of 81.67% over 15 evaluated tag classes. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and trained model checkpoints to foster further research in Marathi NLP.

</details>
